For a few nights I only slept maybe three or four hours, but I’d wake up feeling like I was fine… like I should just use the time and get things done.\n\nDoctor: Did you ever feel so good or hyper that you got into trouble, like spending too much money or doing something reckless?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, not “trouble” like police or anything. The up-and-down part has felt more intense since then.\n\nDoctor: Did these intense periods seem to be triggered by anything in particular, like stressful events or significant changes?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if it was triggered exactly… it’s just been since I moved into the apartment, about a month ago, that the whole up-and-down thing got more intense.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of feeling extremely low or depressed that lasted for two years or more?\n\nPatient: Not two years, no.
